# Buddy Additional Resource Taxonomy Systems

**Status:** `catalogued` subsystem specifications; not evidence of implementation.

This companion catalog expands the primary category systems with additional taxonomies found in DreamCo's resource, source, capability, learning, model-matrix, tool, government-service, and proposed-division registries. Every record below is a generated snapshot with its own stable subsystem ID and section. Similar terms from different source taxonomies remain separate. This document is not a permanent list: new source labels must create new records and sections, while retired or superseded entries retain their history.

- **Current system and group counts:** computed from the machine-readable registry at validation time; neither has a fixed ceiling.
- **Machine-readable source:** `config/buddy/additional-taxonomy-system-registry.json`
- **Published mirror:** `website/data/additional-taxonomy-system-registry.json`
- **Dashboard:** `website/category-systems.html`

All records remain `catalogued`, `not_implemented`, and `not_verified` until their own interfaces, owners, tests, benchmarks, and evidence are delivered.

## Opportunity Category

### benefits

- **Subsystem ID:** `taxonomy_opportunity_category_benefits`
- **Group:** `opportunity_category`
- **Mission:** Dedicated opportunity category subsystem for "benefits".
- **Source files:** `config/buddy-opportunity-resource-registry.json`
- **Source resource IDs:** `benefits-usa`
- **Source record count:** 1
- **Lifecycle:** `catalogued` / `not_implemented` / `not_verified`
- **Inputs:** Exact source labels and identifiers; Source provenance, licensing, and freshness metadata; Approved Buddy task requests
- **Outputs:** Category-specific knowledge and routing metadata; Structured evidence and lifecycle records; Recommendations or governed execution requests
- **Permissions:** Least privilege; No external side effects without required approval
- **Safety controls:** Validate source provenance; Preserve exact labels and source IDs; Expose blocked, stale, and unresolved states; Audit consequential actions
- **Metrics:** Category coverage; Source freshness; Correctness; Reliability; Cost where applicable
- **Acceptance criteria:** Dedicated subsystem ID and category section; Source mappings validated; Owner and interface assigned; Category-specific tests and benchmark/checklist; Failure and rollback behavior documented
- **Dependencies:** buddy_master_orchestrator; buddy_policy_system; buddy_observability_system

## Implementation rule

Every taxonomy subsystem must receive its own owner lane, versioned input/output contract, tests, benchmark or checklist, regression gate, evidence links, and Command Center health state. Shared contract profiles provide reusable defaults only; they do not merge ownership or verification. Any taxonomy term that is proposed, empty, blocked, or unverified must remain visibly so.
